[EVENT: YEM111050]
Date: 2023-08-10
Country: Yemen | Location: Thawkab
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]
Actor2: Military Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council - STC Support and Reinforcement Brigades [State forces]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 10 August 2023, Houthi forces clashed with STC 7th Support and Backup Brigade and 3rd and 5th Southern Resistance Brigades across areas in Thawkab (Al Husha), Sabira in Al Husha district (coded to district center Dawran), Al Jubb (Qaatabah), and Battar Hajar (coded to Al Masharih) (Ad Dhalee, Ad Dali). Clashes took place on 10, 12 and 13 August, resulting in the killing of at least 5 and the injury of 12 Houthi fighters, and the killing of 1 and injury of 11 STC soldiers. 6 fatalities split between 12 events. 1 fatality coded to this event.

[EVENT: YEM81776]
Date: 2023-08-10
Country: Yemen | Location: As Suqayya
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: West Coast Joint Forces [Political militia]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Motabaat; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
Movement of forces: On 10 August 2023, pro-Tareq Saleh forces (coded as Guardians of the Republic under WCJF) deployed near Ras al Arah (coded to As Suqayya) (Al Madaribah Wa Al Arah, Lahij). The deployment followed tensions in the Bab al Mandeb region between Subayhah tribesmen and Tareq Saleh's forces. Tareq Saleh's forces deployed there in late July to establish a maritime brigade before withdrawing after a 72-hour ultimatum from Subayhah tribesmen.
